The order is fixed:\n// the report name, then any --target, --since or --until options, then exactly one log path.\n// Every failure is a *UsageError.\nfunc ParseArgs(args []string) (Options, error) {\n\tif len(args) == 0 {\n\t\treturn Options{}, usageErrorf(\"missing report name\")\n\t}\n\tif !knownReports[args[0]] {\n\t\treturn Options{}, usageErrorf(\"unknown report %q\", args[0])\n\t}\n\topts := Options{Report: args[0]}\n\n\ti := 1\n\tfor ; i < len(args) && strings.HasPrefix(args[i], \"--\"); i += 2 {\n\t\tname := args[i]\n\t\tswitch name {\n\t\tcase \"--target\", \"--since\", \"--until\":\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\treturn Options{}, usageErrorf(\"unknown option %q\", name)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif i+1 >= len(args) {\n\t\t\treturn Options{}, usageErrorf(\"option %s needs a value\", name)\n\t\t}\n\t\tvalue := args[i+1]\n\t\tswitch name {\n\t\tcase \"--target\":\n\t\t\topts.Target = value\n\t\tcase \"--since\":\n\t\t\tif err := checkDate(name, value); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn Options{}, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\topts.Since = value\n\t\tcase \"--until\":\n\t\t\tif err := checkDate(name, value); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn Options{}, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\topts.Until = value\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tpositional := args[i:]\n\tswitch len(positional) {\n\tcase 0:\n\t\treturn Options{}, usageErrorf(\"missing log path\")\n\tcase 1:\n\t\topts.LogPath = positional[0]\n\t\treturn opts, nil\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn Options{}, usageErrorf(\"unexpected argument %q\", positional[1])\n\t}\n}\n---\n// Package logread reads Drydock's JSON Lines LLM execution log into Records.\n//\n// It never writes to the log it reads. Blank lines are ignored; malformed, incomplete and\n// duplicate lines are skipped with a diagnostic on the warning writer.\npackage logread\n\nimport (\n\t\"bufio\"\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n)\n\n// Record is one execution from the log, normalized for reporting.\ntype Record struct {\n\tExecutionID    string\n\tStartedAt      string\n\tDate           string\n\tStatus         string\n\tFailed         bool\n\tCommand        string\n\tTarget         string\n\tProvider       string\n\tRequestedModel string\n\tModel          string\n\tInputTokens    int64\n\tCachedTokens   int64\n\tOutputTokens   int64\n\tCostUSD        float64\n\tElapsedMS      int64\n\tReturnCode     *int\n\tTimedOut       bool\n}\n\n// Skip describes one physical line that Read did not keep.\ntype Skip struct {\n\tLine   int    // 1-based physical line number, counting blank lines\n\tReason string // why the line was skipped\n}\n\n// Result is what Read produced from a log: the decoded records and the skipped lines.\ntype Result struct {\n\tRecords []Record\n\tSkipped int\n\tSkips   []Skip\n}\n\n// wireLine mirrors one JSON Lines object. The required string fields are typed so that a wrong\n// type skips the line. Every other field is kept raw and decoded leniently by parseLine, so a\n// value of the wrong JSON type never costs the whole line.\ntype wireLine struct {\n\tExecutionID string `json:\"execution_id\"`\n\tStartedAt   string `json:\"started_at\"`\n\tStatus      string `json:\"status\"`\n\tJob         struct {\n\t\tCommandName string          `json:\"command_name\"`\n\t\tTarget      string          `json:\"target\"`\n\t\tLLM         json.RawMessage `json:\"llm\"`\n\t\tModel       json.RawMessage `json:\"model\"`\n\t} `json:\"job\"`\n\tResult json.RawMessage `json:\"result\"`\n}\n\n// wireResult mirrors the result object. Each member is raw; see wireLine.\ntype wireResult struct {\n\tReturnCode json.RawMessage `json:\"returncode\"`\n\tTimedOut   json.RawMessage `json:\"timed_out\"`\n\tStats      json.RawMessage `json:\"stats\"`\n}\n\n// wireStats mirrors the result.stats object. Each member is raw; see wireLine.\ntype wireStats struct {\n\tCachedInputTokens json.RawMessage `json:\"cached_input_tokens\"`\n\tElapsedMS         json.RawMessage `json:\"elapsed_ms\"`\n\tInputTokens       json.RawMessage `json:\"input_tokens\"`\n\tOutputTokens      json.RawMessage `json:\"output_tokens\"`\n\tCostUSD           json.RawMessage `json:\"cost_usd\"`\n\tModel             json.RawMessage `json:\"model\"`\n}\n\n// Read decodes every non-blank line of r as one log record. Malformed, incomplete and duplicate\n// lines are counted in Result.Skipped, listed in Result.Skips and described on warn. A duplicate\n// is a later line whose execution_id an earlier kept line already registered. The error is non-nil\n// only when reading from r fails.\nfunc Read(r io.Reader, warn io.Writer) (Result, error) {\n\tif warn == nil {\n\t\twarn = io.Discard\n\t}\n\tvar res Result\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool)\n\tbr := bufio.NewReader(r)\n\tfor lineNo := 1; ; lineNo++ {\n\t\tline, err := br.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif trimmed := bytes.TrimSpace(line); len(trimmed) > 0 {\n\t\t\trec, perr := parseLine(trimmed)\n\t\t\tif perr == nil && seen[rec.ExecutionID] {\n\t\t\t\tperr = errors.New(\"duplicate execution_id\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif perr != nil {\n\t\t\t\tres.Skipped++\n\t\t\t\tres.Skips = append(res.Skips, Skip{Line: lineNo, Reason: perr.Error()})\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(warn, \"line %d: skipped: %v\\n\", lineNo, perr)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tseen[rec.ExecutionID] = true\n\t\t\t\tres.Records = append(res.Records, rec)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {\n\t\t\treturn res, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn res, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// parseLine decodes one non-blank, whitespace-trimmed line into a Record. It fails when the line\n// is not a JSON object or when a required field is absent, empty or not a string. Every other\n// field is optional: an absent, null or wrong-typed value takes its zero value, and a missing\n// result or stats object leaves the record with zero numbers.\n//\n// Model is result.stats.model when that is a non-empty string, otherwise job.model.\nfunc parseLine(data []byte) (Record, error) {\n\tif data[0] != '{' {\n\t\treturn Record{}, errors.New(\"not a JSON object\")\n\t}\n\tvar w wireLine\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &w); err != nil {\n\t\treturn Record{}, err\n\t}\n\trequired := []struct{ name, value string }{\n\t\t{\"execution_id\", w.ExecutionID},\n\t\t{\"started_at\", w.StartedAt},\n\t\t{\"status\", w.Status},\n\t\t{\"job.command_name\", w.Job.CommandName},\n\t\t{\"job.target\", w.Job.Target},\n\t}\n\tfor _, field := range required {\n\t\tif field.value == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn Record{}, fmt.Errorf(\"missing or empty %s\", field.name)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tvar res wireResult\n\tdecodeObject(w.Result, &res)\n\tvar stats wireStats\n\tdecodeObject(res.Stats, &stats)\n\n\trequested := stringValue(w.Job.Model)\n\tmodel := stringValue(stats.Model)\n\tif model == \"\" {\n\t\tmodel = requested\n\t}\n\treturn Record{\n\t\tExecutionID:    w.ExecutionID,\n\t\tStartedAt:      w.StartedAt,\n\t\tDate:           firstRunes(w.StartedAt, 10),\n\t\tStatus:         w.Status,\n\t\tFailed:         w.Status != \"succeeded\",\n\t\tCommand:        w.Job.CommandName,\n\t\tTarget:         w.Job.Target,\n\t\tProvider:       stringValue(w.Job.LLM),\n\t\tRequestedModel: requested,\n\t\tModel:          model,\n\t\tInputTokens:    intValue(stats.InputTokens),\n\t\tCachedTokens:   intValue(stats.CachedInputTokens),\n\t\tOutputTokens:   intValue(stats.OutputTokens),\n\t\tCostUSD:        floatValue(stats.CostUSD),\n\t\tElapsedMS:      intValue(stats.ElapsedMS),\n\t\tReturnCode:     returnCode(res.ReturnCode),\n\t\tTimedOut:       boolValue(res.TimedOut),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// decodeObject decodes raw into v when raw holds a JSON object. Absent, null and non-object values\n// leave v at its zero value.\nfunc decodeObject(raw json.RawMessage, v any) {\n\tif len(raw) > 0 {\n\t\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, v)\n\t}\n}\n\n// intValue returns raw as an int64, or zero when raw is absent, null or not an integer.\nfunc intValue(raw json.RawMessage) int64 {\n\tvar n int64\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &n)\n\treturn n\n}\n\n// floatValue returns raw as a float64, or zero when raw is absent, null or not a number.\nfunc floatValue(raw json.RawMessage) float64 {\n\tvar f float64\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &f)\n\treturn f\n}\n\n// stringValue returns raw as a string, or the empty string when raw is absent, null or not a string.\nfunc stringValue(raw json.RawMessage) string {\n\tvar s string\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &s)\n\treturn s\n}\n\n// boolValue returns raw as a bool, or false when raw is absent, null or not a bool.\nfunc boolValue(raw json.RawMessage) bool {\n\tvar b bool\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &b)\n\treturn b\n}\n\n// returnCode returns raw as a return code. It is nil when raw is absent, null or not an integer.\nfunc returnCode(raw json.RawMessage) *int {\n\tvar n int\n\tif json.Unmarshal(raw, &n) != nil || string(raw) == \"null\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn &n\n}\n\n// firstRunes returns the first n characters of s, or all of s when it is shorter.\nfunc firstRunes(s string, n int) string {\n\tr := []rune(s)\n\tif len(r) > n {\n\t\tr = r[:n]\n\t}\n\treturn string(r)\n}\n---\npackage logread_test\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\n\t\"ddkstat/internal/logread\"\n)\n\n// entry builds one JSON Lines object with the fields Read consumes.\nfunc entry(id, status, startedAt string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(`{\"schema_version\":1,\"execution_id\":%q,\"started_at\":%q,\"status\":%q,`+\n\t\t`\"job\":{\"command_name\":\"build\",\"target\":\"Toml\",\"llm\":\"claude\",\"model\":\"haiku\"},`+\n\t\t`\"result\":{\"returncode\":0,\"timed_out\":false,\"stats\":{\"input_tokens\":10,\"cached_input_tokens\":20,`+\n\t\t`\"output_tokens\":30,\"cost_usd\":0.5,\"elapsed_ms\":40,\"model\":\"claude-haiku-5-5\"}}}`,\n\t\tid, startedAt, status)\n}\n\nfunc read(t *testing.T, input string) logread.Result {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tres, err := logread.Read(strings.NewReader(input), &bytes.Buffer{})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Read: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn res\n}\n\nfunc TestReadBlankLines(t *testing.T) {\n\tinput := \"\\n\" +\n\t\t\"   \\n\" +\n\t\tentry(\"a\", \"succeeded\", \"2026-10-14T06:37:54.176Z\") + \"\\n\" +\n\t\t\"\\t\\n\" +\n\t\t\"\\n\" +\n\t\tentry(\"b\", \"succeeded\", \"2026-10-15T06:37:54.176Z\") + \"\\n\" +\n\t\t\"\\n\"\n\tres := read(t, input)\n\tif len(res.Records) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"records = %d, want 2\", len(res.Records))\n\t}\n\tif res.Records[0].ExecutionID != \"a\" || res.Records[1].ExecutionID != \"b\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"ids = %q, %q; Parsing alone is not\n// enough: time.Parse accepts shapes such as 2026-1-5, so the value must also round-trip exactly.\nfunc checkDate(name, value string) error {\n\tparsed, err := time.Parse(dateLayout, value)\n\tif err != nil || parsed.Format(dateLayout) != value {\n\t\treturn usageErrorf(\"option %s needs a YYYY-MM-DD calendar date, got %q\", name, value)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nconst dateLayout = \"2006-01-02\"\n\n// ParseArgs parses the arguments that follow the program name into Options. Blank lines are ignored; malformed, incomplete and\n// duplicate lines are skipped with a diagnostic on the warning writer.\npackage logread\n\nimport (\n\t\"bufio\"\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n)\n\n// Record is one execution from the log, normalized for reporting.\ntype Record struct {\n\tExecutionID    string\n\tStartedAt      string\n\tDate           string\n\tStatus         string\n\tFailed         bool\n\tCommand        string\n\tTarget         string\n\tProvider       string\n\tRequestedModel string\n\tModel          string\n\tInputTokens    int64\n\tCachedTokens   int64\n\tOutputTokens   int64\n\tCostUSD        float64\n\tElapsedMS      int64\n\tReturnCode     *int\n\tTimedOut       bool\n}\n\n// Skip describes one physical line that Read did not keep.\ntype Skip struct {\n\tLine   int    // 1-based physical line number, counting blank lines\n\tReason string // why the line was skipped\n}\n\n// Result is what Read produced from a log: the decoded records and the skipped lines.\ntype Result struct {\n\tRecords []Record\n\tSkipped int\n\tSkips   []Skip\n}\n\n// wireLine mirrors one JSON Lines object. The required string fields are typed so that a wrong\n// type skips the line. Every other field is kept raw and decoded leniently by parseLine, so a\n// value of the wrong JSON type never costs the whole line.\ntype wireLine struct {\n\tExecutionID string `json:\"execution_id\"`\n\tStartedAt   string `json:\"started_at\"`\n\tStatus      string `json:\"status\"`\n\tJob         struct {\n\t\tCommandName string          `json:\"command_name\"`\n\t\tTarget      string          `json:\"target\"`\n\t\tLLM         json.RawMessage `json:\"llm\"`\n\t\tModel       json.RawMessage `json:\"model\"`\n\t} `json:\"job\"`\n\tResult json.RawMessage `json:\"result\"`\n}\n\n// wireResult mirrors the result object. Each member is raw; see wireLine.\ntype wireResult struct {\n\tReturnCode json.RawMessage `json:\"returncode\"`\n\tTimedOut   json.RawMessage `json:\"timed_out\"`\n\tStats      json.RawMessage `json:\"stats\"`\n}\n\n// wireStats mirrors the result.stats object. Each member is raw; see wireLine.\ntype wireStats struct {\n\tCachedInputTokens json.RawMessage `json:\"cached_input_tokens\"`\n\tElapsedMS         json.RawMessage `json:\"elapsed_ms\"`\n\tInputTokens       json.RawMessage `json:\"input_tokens\"`\n\tOutputTokens      json.RawMessage `json:\"output_tokens\"`\n\tCostUSD           json.RawMessage `json:\"cost_usd\"`\n\tModel             json.RawMessage `json:\"model\"`\n}\n\n// Read decodes every non-blank line of r as one log record. Malformed, incomplete and duplicate\n// lines are counted in Result.Skipped, listed in Result.Skips and described on warn. A duplicate\n// is a later line whose execution_id an earlier kept line already registered. The error is non-nil\n// only when reading from r fails.\nfunc Read(r io.Reader, warn io.Writer) (Result, error) {\n\tif warn == nil {\n\t\twarn = io.Discard\n\t}\n\tvar res Result\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool)\n\tbr := bufio.NewReader(r)\n\tfor lineNo := 1; ; lineNo++ {\n\t\tline, err := br.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif trimmed := bytes.TrimSpace(line); len(trimmed) > 0 {\n\t\t\trec, perr := parseLine(trimmed)\n\t\t\tif perr == nil && seen[rec.ExecutionID] {\n\t\t\t\tperr = errors.New(\"duplicate execution_id\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif perr != nil {\n\t\t\t\tres.Skipped++\n\t\t\t\tres.Skips = append(res.Skips, Skip{Line: lineNo, Reason: perr.Error()})\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(warn, \"line %d: skipped: %v\\n\", lineNo, perr)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tseen[rec.ExecutionID] = true\n\t\t\t\tres.Records = append(res.Records, rec)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {\n\t\t\treturn res, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn res, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// parseLine decodes one non-blank, whitespace-trimmed line into a Record. It fails when the line\n// is not a JSON object or when a required field is absent, empty or not a string. Every other\n// field is optional: an absent, null or wrong-typed value takes its zero value, and a missing\n// result or stats object leaves the record with zero numbers.\n//\n// Model is result.stats.model when that is a non-empty string, otherwise job.model.\nfunc parseLine(data []byte) (Record, error) {\n\tif data[0] != '{' {\n\t\treturn Record{}, errors.New(\"not a JSON object\")\n\t}\n\tvar w wireLine\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &w); err != nil {\n\t\treturn Record{}, err\n\t}\n\trequired := []struct{ name, value string }{\n\t\t{\"execution_id\", w.ExecutionID},\n\t\t{\"started_at\", w.StartedAt},\n\t\t{\"status\", w.Status},\n\t\t{\"job.command_name\", w.Job.CommandName},\n\t\t{\"job.target\", w.Job.Target},\n\t}\n\tfor _, field := range required {\n\t\tif field.value == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn Record{}, fmt.Errorf(\"missing or empty %s\", field.name)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tvar res wireResult\n\tdecodeObject(w.Result, &res)\n\tvar stats wireStats\n\tdecodeObject(res.Stats, &stats)\n\n\trequested := stringValue(w.Job.Model)\n\tmodel := stringValue(stats.Model)\n\tif model == \"\" {\n\t\tmodel = requested\n\t}\n\treturn Record{\n\t\tExecutionID:    w.ExecutionID,\n\t\tStartedAt:      w.StartedAt,\n\t\tDate:           firstRunes(w.StartedAt, 10),\n\t\tStatus:         w.Status,\n\t\tFailed:         w.Status != \"succeeded\",\n\t\tCommand:        w.Job.CommandName,\n\t\tTarget:         w.Job.Target,\n\t\tProvider:       stringValue(w.Job.LLM),\n\t\tRequestedModel: requested,\n\t\tModel:          model,\n\t\tInputTokens:    intValue(stats.InputTokens),\n\t\tCachedTokens:   intValue(stats.CachedInputTokens),\n\t\tOutputTokens:   intValue(stats.OutputTokens),\n\t\tCostUSD:        floatValue(stats.CostUSD),\n\t\tElapsedMS:      intValue(stats.ElapsedMS),\n\t\tReturnCode:     returnCode(res.ReturnCode),\n\t\tTimedOut:       boolValue(res.TimedOut),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// decodeObject decodes raw into v when raw holds a JSON object. Absent, null and non-object values\n// leave v at its zero value.\nfunc decodeObject(raw json.RawMessage, v any) {\n\tif len(raw) > 0 {\n\t\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, v)\n\t}\n}\n\n// intValue returns raw as an int64, or zero when raw is absent, null or not an integer.\nfunc intValue(raw json.RawMessage) int64 {\n\tvar n int64\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &n)\n\treturn n\n}\n\n// floatValue returns raw as a float64, or zero when raw is absent, null or not a number.\nfunc floatValue(raw json.RawMessage) float64 {\n\tvar f float64\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &f)\n\treturn f\n}\n\n// stringValue returns raw as a string, or the empty string when raw is absent, null or not a string.\nfunc stringValue(raw json.RawMessage) string {\n\tvar s string\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &s)\n\treturn s\n}\n\n// boolValue returns raw as a bool, or false when raw is absent, null or not a bool.\nfunc boolValue(raw json.RawMessage) bool {\n\tvar b bool\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &b)\n\treturn b\n}\n\n// returnCode returns raw as a return code.
